Title: The Liturgy of the New Church, signified by the New Jerusalem in the Revelation; together with the forms for the administration of Baptism and the Holy Supper; and a Catechism for the use of the New Church; also, Hymns and Spiritual Songs, by the Rev. Mr. Joseph Proud, Minister of the New Church; the fourth edition
Creator: The New Church (New Jerusalem Church); hymns by Joseph Proud
Publisher: Baltimore: printed and sold by Samuel and John Adams, in Market-Street
Publication Date: 1793
Rights: Public Domain (published 1793, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item liturgyour00newj (https://archive.org/details/liturgyour00newj), the item's own liturgyour00newj_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Princeton Theological Seminary Library; host metadata date: 1792. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: da60c3108d4118cf59f3389eb1048bcfe5c8a043a45066f0c7c1a88a56b182b0.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Princeton Theological Seminary scan, from the library of Louis FitzGerald Benson; usable OCR, the spine and cover garbled, the text readable with the long s as f. The host dates the item 1792; the title page reads 1793. A note in the preface says the third edition, printed in 1790 by Robert Hindmarsh in London, was 'calculated for the Kingdom of Great Britain only', and that this copy is altered for the United States.
Content note: The New Church's order of worship as adopted in America: the liturgy with its confession to the Lord Jesus 'in his Divine Humanity', the forms for baptism and the holy supper, the catechism, and Proud's hymns, including hymns against the Calvinist doctrine.

PREFACE. 

IT  is  written  in  the  Revelation,  "  I  faw  a 
"  New  Heaven  and  a  New  Earth  ;  for  the 
"  former  Heaven  and  the  former  Earth  were 
"  patted  away.  And  I  faw  the  Holy  City,  New 
"  Jerufalem,  coming  down  from  God  out  of 
"  Heaven,  prepared  as  a  Bride  adorned  for  her 
"  Hufband."  By  thefe  Words  is  not  meant  that 
the  vifible  Heavens  and  habitable  Earth  fhall 
be  diffolved,  and  a  New  Heaven  and  New  Earth 
created,  and  that  the  holy  City  Jerufalem  will  de- 
fcend  upon  the  New  Earth,  according  to  the 
literal  Conftruction  ;  but  by  the  New  Heaven 
and  the  New  Earth  is  meant  a  New  Church  both 
in  Heaven  and  on  Earth  ;  and  by  the  New  Je- 
rufalem defcending  from  God  out  of  Heaven,  is 
fignified  the  heavenly  Doctrine  of  that  Church, 
revealed  by  the  Lord  himfelf.  This  likewife  is 
what  is  fignified  by  the  Second  Coming  of  the 
Lord,  which  confifteth,  not  in  a  perfonal  Ap- 
pearance upon  the  Earth,  but  in  the  Revelation 
of  the  internal  or  fpiritual  Senfe  of  the  Holy 
Word,  whereby  the  human  Mind  is  now  capa- 
ble of  feeing  and  underftanding  the  fpiritual 
Truths  therein  contained  in  a  rational  Manner. 
The  Doctrines  contained  in  the  following 
Form  of  Prayer  are  the  Doctrines  of  the  New 
Church,  being  taken  from  the  Theological  Writ- 
ings of  the  Hon.  Emanuel  Swedenborgh, 
and  when  impartially  and  attentively  examined, 
will  be  found  in  Agreement  with  the  genuine 
Truth  of  the  Holy  Scripture  or  Word  of  God. 
It  is  not,  however,  expected  that  this  Form  ©f

Prayer  mould  be  confidered  as  perfecT:  or  com-

in  Eflencc  and  in  Perfon,  in  whom  is  a  Divine

God.  ^  Thefe   two  Effentials   and  Univerfals  of 
Doctrine  enter  into  every  Particular  of  the  New

that  the  various  Modes  of  worshipping  the  Lord

Three  Perfons  in  the  Godhead,  or  in  other  Words,
